# Income Limits: Rhode Island

- Postal code: RI
- State FIPS: 44
- Counties: 0
- Towns: 39

HUD's Section 8 income limits for Rhode Island, fiscal years 1998 to 2026: 39 towns, as HUD lists them in FY2026. Before FY2006 HUD keyed New England's limits by county, not town: those rows are kept below, under Counties before 2006, and each town names its own as `County before 2006`.
